Help me choose
Frequently asked questions
Contact
Who is Parkezi designed for?
Parkezi is ideal for parking operators managing parking lots in commercial complexes, hotels, airports, shopping malls, hospitals, and universities. It also serves individuals and corporate clients looking for parking space optimisation.
How is Parkezi different to other parking systems?
We have designed Parkezi to be affordable, flexible simple to use and above all, reliable. We can often integrate Parkezi with existing carpark hardware as well as existing in-house software you may be using. Parkezi has an easy to use browser based interface that gives you a fast and easy way to configure and update your system and monitor it in real time. 100% of revenue goes direct to your bank accounts (less transaction processing fees).
Can I integrate Parkezi with existing carpark hardware or software systems?
Yes, we can usually integrate Parkezi with existing carpark hardware including ANPR cameras, boom gates and sliding gates. Parkezi has an open API that allows it to communicate with many other software packages that also support open API communication.
What geographic locations can you currently service?
We are able to install systems in most locations across Australia and New Zealand, with South East Asia support to be added in the not too distant future.
Are you able to manage our carpark for us?
Yes, we can do this if needed. We can answer queries from the carpark intercom and resolve customer or payment issues 24 hours a day without you needing to be involved.
What features does the Parkezi software offer?
The software provides an easy to use interface to configure access control whitelists, time of day pricing structures, multiple zone access control and reporting on revenue, car park activity and other useful data.
What backup options are available?
Backup connectivity and UPS power can be arranged for the site. Discuss your network, power supply and operating requirements with PARKEZI to confirm an appropriate configuration.
What payment methods are available to carpark customers?
Our 'Express Pay' and 'Step & Pay' terminals provide an easy and convenient way for customers to pay for their parking. It is also possible for them to pay you directly and then for their car numberplate to be flagged for instant exit from the carpark. You can also offer customers the ability to pay via a web based browser interface from their mobile or even use a QR code to allow them to exit the carpark.
What reseller opportunities are available?
We have a reseller program for access and gate automation companies to resell ParkEzi. Contact us for more details.
What is ticketless parking?
Tickless parking refers to a system where drivers can park their vehicles without the need to obtain a physical ticket or permit. Instead of traditional tickets, the system uses technology like automatic number plate recognition (ANPR) to identify vehicles as they enter and leave parking facilities.
Here's a simplified way it usually works:
Entry: As the vehicle enters the parking lot, its license plate is scanned logging the vehicle's entry time.
Duration: The parking system monitors how long the vehicle remains in the parking facility.
Exit: When the vehicle leaves, its exit time is recorded using the same identification method. The system calculates the parking fee based on the duration of the stay.
Payment: Drivers pay for their parking time via a credit card reader or online payment system.
What payment options are available for purchasing Parkezi?
We offer two options:
Purchase Outright: You can buy the system outright with a one-time payment.
Rent-to-Own: You can rent the system with a plan that allows you to own it after 24–36 months.
Where does the revenue go?
With Parkezi, 100% of the revenue is yours. We configure your payment systems so that all revenue is deposited directly into your account. We don't take any percentage.
Permanent parkers vs casual parkers
Permanent Parkers: Individuals who park regularly or long-term, typically holding monthly or annual passes for consistent access. Examples include employees of nearby businesses or residents with reserved parking spaces. This is managed via a cloud-based portal by the parking management company.
Casual Parkers: Individuals who park occasionally, paying for each visit based on time spent parked. Parkezi software can calculate time spent onsite and then charge using a dynamic pricing structure.

# Automated Parking Systems

Parkezi®

Paid Parking

![Airplane parked at airport gate, with another plane taking off against a colorful sunset sky.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/PaidParkingBG_1.avif)

Designed for parking environments requiring payment integration .

Manage parking fees seamlessly with ticketless stations, multiple payment gateways and remote access.

A customisable whitelist system allows you full control of access and revenue collection via ANPR cameras, QR codes or NFC swipe cards. This is ideal for mixed-use car parks where visitors pay for access and staff or residents gain free entry.

Keep 100% of your revenue – with traditional systems, you would pay an external company a commission for managing your car park. With PARKEZI®, you can purchase or lease the system and collect all the parking revenue.

[1300 309 9981300 309 998](tel:1300309998) [EnquireEnquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

![A modern, large indoor shopping mall with glass railings and metal columns.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/OneWayShoppingCentre.avif)

Parkezi **®**

Two Way

Ideal for car parks requiring controlled access, such as gated communities or office complexes.

![A hotel staff member pushes a luggage cart as a couple checks in at the front desk.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/TwoWayBG_1.avif)

ANPR cameras scan the license plate, verifying it against a cloud-based whitelist to log entry and exit times.

Enhances traffic flow and security with ANPR-enabled gates and simple software integration.

Can operate a variety of barriers, including security gates, boom gates or underground garage doors.

Displays real-time car park occupancy, providing live car counts and better traffic management.

[1300 309 9981300 309 998](tel:1300309998) [EnquireEnquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

![Empty underground parking lot with marked spaces and green pillars.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/Two-Way.avif)

Parkezi **®**

One Way

**A straightforward solution focused on entry access control.**

![](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/OneWayBG_1.avif)

ANPR cameras scan the license plate, verifying it against a cloud-based whitelist to log entry and exit times.

An ANPR whitelist system allows vehicles to enter and upon exit, an induction loop opens the gate when it detects the vehicle’s presence.

A ticketless and simplified system, minimising bottlenecks and making it a hands-free experience for drivers.

A cost-effective solution for locations with less complex traffic flow needs.

[1300 309 9981300 309 998](tel:1300309998) [EnquireEnquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

# Installation

Take the hard out of hardware installation

[1300 309 9981300 309 998](tel:1300309998) [EnquireEnquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

## Professional Parking System Installation Across Australia

A parking system is only as good as its installation.

You can invest in the best ANPR cameras, cloud software and payment technology available, but if the system is not installed correctly, performance will suffer. Delays, connectivity issues and misaligned hardware can quickly undermine what should be a smooth experience.

At Parkezi, installation is treated as a critical stage of the project, not an afterthought. We work closely with property managers, developers, councils and commercial operators to ensure every parking automation system is installed properly, tested thoroughly and configured to suit the site.

## A Practical, Structured Approach to Installation

Every car park is different. Layout, traffic flow, power supply, connectivity and user behaviour all influence how the system should be deployed.

That is why our installation process starts with a proper assessment. This allows us to design a parking automation system that works with your site, not against it.

We look at:

- Entry and exit camera positioning
- Traffic volume and peak flow
- Existing boom gates or infrastructure
- Power and network access
- Signage and driver visibility

If you are exploring ANPR parking systems or ticketless parking solutions, installation planning is where everything begins.

[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

![Parkezi column gate unit with red LED access indicator.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/ep-why-5.avif)

## ANPR Camera Installation Done Right

Automatic Number Plate Recognition is highly accurate, but correct positioning is essential.

Cameras must be:

- Aligned precisely
- Mounted at the correct height
- Protected from glare and obstruction
- Connected to a reliable and stable network

Poor placement can reduce recognition accuracy and create unnecessary frustration. If your car park is going to support motorcycles which only have rear-facing number plates, then additional cameras may be required.

Parkezi’s team ensures ANPR camera installation is configured to maximise reliability, even in high-traffic or challenging environments.

[Learn about our ANPR systems](https://www.parkezi.com.au/systems/)

![Parkezi ANPR camera installed at a car park entry point.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/ep-why-3.avif)

## Boom Gate and Access Control Installation

Whether you are installing fixed access points or a portable solution like SolarGate+, proper setup ensures smooth daily operation.

Installation includes:

- Secure boom gate mounting
- Safe vehicle clearance positioning
- Integration with ANPR cameras
- Calibration of opening and closing timing
- Safety testing and fail-safe configuration

We also ensure your parking access control system integrates seamlessly with your payment and cloud management platform.

[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

![Indoor Express Pay parking payment terminal.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/indoor-express-pay.webp)

## Cloud Configuration and Software Setup

Modern parking management software is cloud-based. That means installation is not just physical, it is digital.

We configure:

- Site rules and pricing structures
- Access permissions
- Time-based controls
- Reporting dashboards
- Payment integrations

This ensures your parking management software in Australia is fully operational from day one. Because Parkezi’s systems are cloud-connected, multi-site operators also benefit from centralised visibility and simplified control.

[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

![Parkezi info board displaying site rules and parking rates.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/ep-why-4.avif)

## Testing Before Go-Live

Before a system is handed over, we conduct thorough testing.

This includes:

- Entry and exit accuracy
- Payment confirmation flow
- Data logging verification
- Gate response timing
- Contingency procedures

We simulate real-world usage to ensure the experience is smooth for drivers and manageable for operators.

The goal is simple. When your system goes live, it should work exactly as expected.

![Driver using contactless payment at a Parkezi terminal during system testing.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/ep-why-1.avif)

Site Types

## Installation for  Different Site Types

Parkezi installs systems across a wide range of environments. Each environment has unique operational needs. Installation is tailored accordingly.

Shopping Centres

Hotels

Residential Complexes

Commercial Buildings

Construction Sites

Event Parking

Council-Managed Facilities

Residential sites prioritise secure access and visitor control. Retail sites prioritise fast throughput and reduced congestion. Construction sites often require flexible, mobile boom gate systems.

We adjust the approach to match the use case. From a single entry residential site to a multi-storey car park, the installation process is thorough, professional and backed by Parkezi’s expertise.

## Minimising Disruption During Installation

We understand that most car parks cannot simply close for extended periods.

Our installation planning focuses on:

- Clear communication with site managers
- Staged rollouts where required
- Minimal interruption to daily operations
- Safe traffic management during works

Where possible, installations are scheduled to reduce impact during peak periods.

[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

![Vehicle exiting through a Parkezi boom gate.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/ep-why-2.avif)

## Ongoing Support After Installation

Installation is not the end of the relationship.

Once your parking automation system is operational, Parkezi provides ongoing support, system monitoring and maintenance guidance.

Because the system is cloud-connected, updates and adjustments can often be handled remotely, reducing downtime and service costs.

[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

![Parkezi management box for ongoing support.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/parkezi-box.webp)

## Frequently Asked Questions

How long does parking system installation take?

Timeframes depend on site size and complexity. Smaller sites may be completed quickly, while larger multi-entry facilities require staged installation.

Can existing boom gates be reused?

In many cases, yes. We assess existing infrastructure to determine compatibility and suitability.

Do you provide installation across Australia?

Yes. Parkezi supports installations anywhere in Australia.

Will installation disrupt daily operations?

We aim to minimise disruption through careful scheduling and staged implementation where necessary.

## Ready to Install a Smarter  Parking System?

Whether you are upgrading an existing car park or building a new facility, correct installation ensures your investment performs reliably from day one. Parkezi combines practical installation expertise with advanced parking automation technology to deliver systems that are secure, scalable and easy to manage. If you are planning a new installation, speak with our team to discuss your site requirements.

# About Parkezi

## **Every car park is unique. Your parking system should be too**

We design and deliver custom-built parking software that can be configured and developed to suit your site’s unique challenges. Not force you into a rigid, off the shelf system.

Whether you need specialised access rules, tailored validation workflows, integrations with third-party platforms, or a completely bespoke operating model, Parkezi gives you the flexibility to build a solution that fits your environment now and evolves as your needs change.

![A person uses a smartphone to make a contactless payment at a parking payment machine.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/1_1.avif)

## Technology That Works With You

We work alongside property owners, facility managers, and parking operators to deliver solutions that improve day-to-day operations without disrupting established workflows.

‍

**Our platform combines:**

- High-accuracy ANPR technology (99.9%)
- Cloud-based management, reporting and remote access (no onsite servers required)
- Flexible pricing, custom rules for registered users and booking systems
- Custom tarriff rules & Happy Hours (free parking) tailored to each site
- Scalable integrations across hardware and third-party systems
- Multiple payment options, including app-less QR code payments ideal for smaller car parks

‍

**Because Parkezi is built in-house, we can tailor workflows, rules and integrations to match each site’s unique requirements.**

‍

We also offer **portable boom gate access solutions for events or work sites**, with **fully integrated payments** for temporary or pop-up parking environments.

‍

All supported by an Australian-based team that understands both software and the physical realities of parking infrastructure.

![A car with bright headlights drives through a parking lot barrier gate.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/2_1.avif)

## Designed for Real Parking Environments

Parking isn’t just about entry and exit.

It’s about flow, compliance, reporting, fixing lost revenue, security, and user experience.

‍

Because when parking is done right, drivers simply **pull in and pull out with zero friction**.

‍

No confusion. No bottlenecks. No second-guessing.

‍

The experience should be so seamless that customers don’t even think about the parking.

‍

All they remember is that visiting your site was easy, and they’re more likely to come back.

‍

**Parkezi is designed to support:**

- Hotels and mixed-use developments
- Commercial buildings & garages
- Hospitals and medical precincts
- Container yards and logistics sites
- Residential complexes
- Retail centres, event venues and high-turnover sites

‍

No two sites are identical, and that’s exactly how we approach every project.

## Our Philosophy

We believe parking systems should be:

‍

- Reliable
- Adaptable
- Effortless
- Operator-friendly
- Built to evolve

‍

‍

As sites grow and requirements change, Parkezi evolves with them.

Using our advanced cloud-based software platform and leading Automatic Number Plate Recognition (ANPR) technology, lost tickets, bottlenecks, complex user interfaces and unauthorised access are a thing of the past. And unlike traditional systems, PARKEZI® gives you full control over revenue and data collection.

No matter your business size and requirements, PARKEZI® will take the pain out of the process and put your parking management into auto-drive. Too easy.

[What is ticketless parking?](https://www.parkezi.com.au/about-us/#)

Tickless parking refers to a system where drivers can park their vehicles without the need to obtain a physical ticket or permit. Instead of traditional tickets, the system uses technology like automatic number plate recognition (ANPR) to identify vehicles as they enter and leave parking facilities.

**Here's a simplified way it usually works:**

**Entry:** As the vehicle enters the parking lot, its license plate is scanned logging the vehicle's entry time.

**Duration:** The parking system monitors how long the vehicle remains in the parking facility.

**Exit:** When the vehicle leaves, its exit time is recorded using the same identification method. The system calculates the parking fee based on the duration of the stay.

**Payment:** Drivers pay for their parking time via a credit card reader or online payment system

[1300 309 9981300 309 998](tel:1300309998) [EnquireEnquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

[What payment options are available for purchasing Parkezi?](https://www.parkezi.com.au/about-us/#)

We offer two options:

**Purchase Outright:** You can buy the system outright with a one-time payment. **Rent-to-Own:** You can rent the system with a plan that allows you to own it after 24-36 months.

[1300 309 9981300 309 998](tel:1300309998) [EnquireEnquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

[Where does the revenue go?](https://www.parkezi.com.au/about-us/#)

With Parkezi, **100% of the revenue is yours**. We configure your payment systems so that all revenue is deposited directly into your account. We don't take any percentage.

[1300 309 9981300 309 998](tel:1300309998) [EnquireEnquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

[Permanent parkers vs casual parkers](https://www.parkezi.com.au/about-us/#)

**Permanent Parkers:** Individuals who park regularly or long-term, typically holding monthly or annual passes for consistent access. Examples include employees of nearby businesses or residents with reserved parking spaces. This is managed via a cloud-based portal by the parking management company.

‍ **Casual Parkers:** Individuals who park occasionally, paying for each visit based on time spent parked. Parkezi software can calculate time spent onsite and then charge using a dynamic pricing structure

# Frequently Asked Questions

Who is Parkezi designed for?

Parkezi is ideal for parking operators managing parking lots in commercial complexes, hotels, airports, shopping malls, hospitals, and universities. It also serves individuals and corporate clients looking for parking space optimisation.

How is Parkezi different to other parking systems?

We have designed Parkezi to be affordable, flexible simple to use and above all, reliable. We can often integrate Parkezi with existing carpark hardware as well as existing in-house software you may be using. Parkezi has an easy to use browser based interface that gives you a fast and easy way to configure and update your system and monitor it in real time. 100% of revenue goes direct to your bank accounts (less transaction processing fees).

Can I integrate Parkezi with existing carpark hardware or software systems?

Yes, we can usually integrate Parkezi with existing carpark hardware including ANPR cameras, boom gates and sliding gates. Parkezi has an open API that allows it to communicate with many other software packages that also support open API communication.

What geographic locations can you currently service?

We are able to install systems in most locations across Australia and New Zealand, with South East Asia support to be added in the not too distant future.

Are you able to manage our carpark for us?

Yes, we can do this if needed. We can answer queries from the carpark intercom and resolve customer or payment issues 24 hours a day without you needing to be involved.

What features does the Parkezi software offer?

The software provides an easy to use interface to configure access control whitelists, time of day pricing structures, multiple zone access control and reporting on revenue, car park activity and other useful data.

How reliable is Parkezi?

Our software runs on an industrial PC with GSM data backup located at your facility, so even if your internet goes down, your carpark will still function. UPS backup power can also be arranged. All customer data is stored on secure and reliable Australian based servers with 99.98% uptime.

What payment methods are available to carpark customers?

Our ' [Express Pay](https://www.parkezi.com.au/products/express-pay/)' and ' [Step and Go](https://www.parkezi.com.au/products/scan-and-go/)' terminals provide an easy and convenient way for customers to pay for their parking. It is also possible for them to pay you directly and then for their car numberplate to be flagged for instant exit from the carpark. You can also offer customers the ability to pay via a web based browser interface from their mobile or even use a QR code to allow them to exit the carpark.

What reseller opportunities are available?

We have a reseller program for access and gate automation companies to resell ParkEzi. Contact us for more details.

What is ticketless parking?

Tickless parking refers to a system where drivers can park their vehicles without the need to obtain a physical ticket or permit. Instead of traditional tickets, the system uses technology like automatic number plate recognition (ANPR) to identify vehicles as they enter and leave parking facilities.

**Here's a simplified way it usually works:**

**Entry:** As the vehicle enters the parking lot, its license plate is scanned logging the vehicle's entry time.

**Duration:** The parking system monitors how long the vehicle remains in the parking facility.

**Exit:** When the vehicle leaves, its exit time is recorded using the same identification method. The system calculates the parking fee based on the duration of the stay.

**Payment:** Drivers pay for their parking time via a credit card reader or online payment system.

What payment options are available for purchasing Parkezi?

We offer two options:

**Purchase Outright:** You can buy the system outright with a one-time payment.

**Rent-to-Own:** You can rent the system with a plan that allows you to own it after 24–36 months.

Where does the revenue go?

With Parkezi, **100% of the revenue is yours**. We configure your payment systems so that all revenue is deposited directly into your account. We don't take any percentage.

Permanent parkers vs casual parkers

**Permanent Parkers:** Individuals who park regularly or long-term, typically holding monthly or annual passes for consistent access. Examples include employees of nearby businesses or residents with reserved parking spaces. This is managed via a cloud-based portal by the parking management company.

**Casual Parkers:** Individuals who park occasionally, paying for each visit based on time spent parked. Parkezi software can calculate time spent onsite and then charge using a dynamic pricing structure.

Answers to common questions about PARKEZI hardware, software and support.
Who is PARKEZI for?
PARKEZI is ideal for parking operators managing parking lots in commercial complexes, hotels, airports, shopping malls, hospitals, and universities. It also serves individuals and corporate clients looking for parking space optimisation.
How is PARKEZI different?

PARKEZI has an easy to use browser based interface that gives you a fast and easy way to configure and update your system and monitor it in real time. 100% of revenue goes direct to your bank accounts (less transaction processing fees).
Can existing equipment be used?
Yes, we can usually integrate PARKEZI with existing carpark hardware including ANPR cameras, boom gates and sliding gates. PARKEZI has an open API that allows it to communicate with many other software packages that also support open API communication.
Where can you install?
PARKEZI installs systems in most locations across Australia and New Zealand. Contact the team to discuss your location.
Can you manage our car park?
Yes, we can do this if needed. We can answer queries from the carpark intercom and resolve customer or payment issues 24 hours a day without you needing to be involved.
What can the software do?
The software provides an easy to use interface to configure access control whitelists, time of day pricing structures, multiple zone access control and reporting on revenue, car park activity and other useful data.
What backup options are available?
Backup connectivity and UPS power can be arranged for the site. Discuss your network, power supply and operating requirements with PARKEZI to confirm an appropriate configuration.
How can drivers pay?
Our 'Express Pay' and 'Step & Pay' terminals provide an easy and convenient way for customers to pay for their parking. It is also possible for them to pay you directly and then for their car numberplate to be flagged for instant exit from the carpark. You can also offer customers the ability to pay via a web based browser interface from their mobile or even use a QR code to allow them to exit the carpark.
Can I become a reseller?
We have a reseller program for access and gate automation companies to resell PARKEZI. Contact us for more details.
What is ticketless parking?
Ticketless parking refers to a system where drivers can park their vehicles without the need to obtain a physical ticket or permit. Instead of traditional tickets, the system uses technology like automatic number plate recognition (ANPR) to identify vehicles as they enter and leave parking facilities.
Here's a simplified way it usually works:
Entry: As the vehicle enters the parking lot, its license plate is scanned logging the vehicle's entry time.
Duration: The parking system monitors how long the vehicle remains in the parking facility.
Exit: When the vehicle leaves, its exit time is recorded using the same identification method. The system calculates the parking fee based on the duration of the stay.
Payment: Drivers pay for their parking time via a credit card reader or online payment system.
How can I buy a system?
We offer two options:
Purchase Outright: You can buy the system outright with a one-time payment.
Rent-to-Own: You can rent the system with a plan that allows you to own it after 24 to 36 months.
Where does the revenue go?
Parking revenue is deposited directly into your account. PARKEZI does not take a percentage of parking revenue. Payment processing fees still apply.
Permanent or casual parking?
Permanent Parkers: Individuals who park regularly or long-term, typically holding monthly or annual passes for consistent access. Examples include employees of nearby businesses or residents with reserved parking spaces. This is managed via a cloud-based portal by the parking management company.
Casual Parkers: Individuals who park occasionally, paying for each visit based on time spent parked. PARKEZI software can calculate time spent onsite and then charge using a dynamic pricing structure.

## How It Works

## Fast, Ticketless Parking Payments Without the Queues

Nobody enjoys lining up at a payment machine after a long day. Drivers want to enter, park, pay and leave without delay. Express Pay by Parkezi is designed to make that happen.

Express Pay is a ticketless parking system that simplifies payment while keeping traffic moving. It removes the need for paper tickets and bulky payment kiosks, replacing them with a faster, smarter, cloud-connected solution.

Whether you manage a shopping centre, hotel, hospital or commercial site, Express Pay helps you deliver a smoother experience for drivers while improving revenue accuracy behind the scenes.

[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/products/express-pay#contact)

![Parkezi Express Pay unit.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/1_1.avif)

## How Express Pay Works

Express Pay integrates with Parkezi’s ANPR parking system to recognise vehicles on entry and exit.

When a vehicle enters your car park:

- The number plate is captured automatically
- The user’s parking session has started
- The boom gate opens for them to enter

Before returning to their vehicle, drivers can pay by either:

- Using the on-foot pay station (Step & Pay)
- Scanning a QR code and paying via our app-less Parkezi Pay system

Drivers can also complete payment instantly using contactless tap-and-go at the exit terminal. Once payment is confirmed, exit is seamless.

[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/products/express-pay#contact)

![Parkezi unit at car park exit.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/2_1.avif)

No ticket to lose.

No queues at machines.

No manual reconciliation.

## Just efficient, fully ticketless parking.

Ideal For

## Designed for Modern  Australian Car Parks

Express Pay is ideal for high-traffic environments where speed and reliability matter. It works particularly well for:

Retail and shopping centres

Hotels and hospitality venues

Health facilities

Commercial office buildings

Mixed-use developments

By removing traditional ticket hardware, you also reduce maintenance costs and downtime.

If you are already using Parkezi’s parking automation system, Express Pay integrates seamlessly into your existing setup.

## Why Operators Choose Express Pay

![Vehicle exiting through Parkezi boom gate.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/ep-why-2.avif)

### Faster Exit Flow

Express Pay removes the need for physical tickets. On exit, the vehicle’s licence plate is automatically scanned and payment is verified in real time. If parking hasn’t been prepaid, drivers can simply use contactless tap-and-go at the exit terminal. No tickets. No searching. No delays.

![Parkezi ANPR camera for parking access control.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/camera.webp)

### Lower Hardware Maintenance

Traditional pay stations require servicing, receipt rolls and frequent repairs. Express Pay reduces reliance on heavy machinery and is completely paperless.

![Parkezi info board showing parking rates and entry time.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/ep-why-4.avif)

### Accurate Revenue Reporting

Every transaction is logged in real time through Parkezi’s cloud parking management software, giving you complete visibility.

![Driver tapping phone to pay at Parkezi terminal.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/ep-why-1.avif)

### Flexible Payment Options

Drivers can choose how they want to pay, including mobile-friendly and contactless options.

## Part of a Smarter Parking Ecosystem

Express Pay is not a standalone product. It forms part of a complete parking management software solution in Australia.

When combined with:

- ANPR cameras
- Parking analytics and reporting
- Cloud-based management dashboards

You gain a fully integrated parking access control system that improves performance across your entire site.

[Learn more about Parkezi’s full parking systems](https://www.parkezi.com.au/systems/)

![Parkezi smart parking ecosystem.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/smarter-parking.webp)

## Built for Scalability

Whether you manage one site or multiple locations across Brisbane, Melbourne or Sydney, Express Pay can scale with your operation.

Because it is cloud-based, updates and reporting are centralised. That means consistent performance across every location.

[View Case Studies](https://www.parkezi.com.au/our-work/)

![Indoor Express Pay parking payment terminal.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/indoor-express-pay.webp)

## Frequently Asked Questions

Is Express Pay a ticketless parking system?

Yes. Express Pay eliminates paper tickets by securely linking all payments directly to a vehicle’s licence plate using ANPR technology.

Does Express Pay support contactless payments?

Yes. Drivers can complete payment using contactless tap-and-go or supported mobile payment methods.

Does Express Pay require servicing?

Maintenance requirements are minimal. With no moving mechanical parts, wear and tear is significantly reduced compared to traditional ticketing systems.

General inspections are recommended to ensure optimal performance. The enclosure includes three intake cooling fans to regulate internal temperature in all weather conditions. The air filter should be cleaned or replaced annually as part of routine maintenance.

Is it suitable for busy car parks?

Absolutely. Express Pay is designed for high-traffic environments, reducing congestion and improving vehicle throughput during peak periods.

Express Pay questions
Is Express Pay a ticketless parking system?
Yes. Express Pay eliminates paper tickets by securely linking all payments directly to a vehicle’s licence plate using ANPR technology.
Does Express Pay support contactless payments?
Yes. Drivers can complete payment using contactless tap-and-go or supported mobile payment methods.
Does Express Pay require servicing?
Maintenance requirements are minimal. With no moving mechanical parts, wear and tear is significantly reduced compared to traditional ticketing systems. General inspections are recommended to ensure optimal performance. The enclosure includes three intake cooling fans to regulate internal temperature in all weather conditions. The air filter should be cleaned or replaced annually as part of routine maintenance.
Is it suitable for busy car parks?
Absolutely. Express Pay is designed for high-traffic environments, reducing congestion and improving vehicle throughput during peak periods.
Who is PARKEZI for?
PARKEZI is ideal for parking operators managing parking lots in commercial complexes, hotels, airports, shopping malls, hospitals, and universities. It also serves individuals and corporate clients looking for parking space optimisation.
How is PARKEZI different?
We have designed PARKEZI to be affordable, flexible simple to use and above all, reliable. We can often integrate PARKEZI with existing carpark hardware as well as existing in-house software you may be using. PARKEZI has an easy to use browser based interface that gives you a fast and easy way to configure and update your system and monitor it in real time. 100% of revenue goes direct to your bank accounts (less transaction processing fees).
Can existing equipment be used?
Yes, we can usually integrate PARKEZI with existing carpark hardware including ANPR cameras, boom gates and sliding gates. PARKEZI has an open API that allows it to communicate with many other software packages that also support open API communication.
Where can you install?
PARKEZI installs systems in most locations across Australia and New Zealand. Contact the team to discuss your location.
Can you manage our car park?
Yes, we can do this if needed. We can answer queries from the carpark intercom and resolve customer or payment issues 24 hours a day without you needing to be involved.
What can the software do?
The software provides an easy to use interface to configure access control whitelists, time of day pricing structures, multiple zone access control and reporting on revenue, car park activity and other useful data.
What backup options are available?
Backup connectivity and UPS power can be arranged for the site. Discuss your network, power supply and operating requirements with PARKEZI to confirm an appropriate configuration.
How can drivers pay?
Our 'Express Pay' and 'Step & Pay' terminals provide an easy and convenient way for customers to pay for their parking. It is also possible for them to pay you directly and then for their car numberplate to be flagged for instant exit from the carpark. You can also offer customers the ability to pay via a web based browser interface from their mobile or even use a QR code to allow them to exit the carpark.
Can I become a reseller?
We have a reseller program for access and gate automation companies to resell PARKEZI. Contact us for more details.
What is ticketless parking?
Ticketless parking refers to a system where drivers can park their vehicles without the need to obtain a physical ticket or permit. Instead of traditional tickets, the system uses technology like automatic number plate recognition (ANPR) to identify vehicles as they enter and leave parking facilities.
Here's a simplified way it usually works:
Entry: As the vehicle enters the parking lot, its license plate is scanned logging the vehicle's entry time.
Duration: The parking system monitors how long the vehicle remains in the parking facility.
Exit: When the vehicle leaves, its exit time is recorded using the same identification method. The system calculates the parking fee based on the duration of the stay.
Payment: Drivers pay for their parking time via a credit card reader or online payment system.
How can I buy a system?
We offer two options:
Purchase Outright: You can buy the system outright with a one-time payment.
Rent-to-Own: You can rent the system with a plan that allows you to own it after 24 to 36 months.
Where does the revenue go?
Parking revenue is deposited directly into your account. PARKEZI does not take a percentage of parking revenue. Payment processing fees still apply.
Permanent or casual parking?
Permanent Parkers: Individuals who park regularly or long-term, typically holding monthly or annual passes for consistent access. Examples include employees of nearby businesses or residents with reserved parking spaces. This is managed via a cloud-based portal by the parking management company.
Casual Parkers: Individuals who park occasionally, paying for each visit based on time spent parked. PARKEZI software can calculate time spent onsite and then charge using a dynamic pricing structure.

Step & Pay questions
Which Step & Pay models can I choose?
Choose the standard unit or larger-screen unit using the model selector. Contact PARKEZI to confirm the screen dimensions and configuration for your site.
Where can Step & Pay be installed?
Place the unit in convenient locations such as foyers or lift wells, allowing drivers to pay before returning to their vehicle.
Does it work with Express Pay?
Yes. Step & Pay is an on-foot payment station used alongside Express Pay.
Who is PARKEZI for?
PARKEZI is ideal for parking operators managing parking lots in commercial complexes, hotels, airports, shopping malls, hospitals, and universities. It also serves individuals and corporate clients looking for parking space optimisation.
How is PARKEZI different?
We have designed PARKEZI to be affordable, flexible simple to use and above all, reliable. We can often integrate PARKEZI with existing carpark hardware as well as existing in-house software you may be using. PARKEZI has an easy to use browser based interface that gives you a fast and easy way to configure and update your system and monitor it in real time. 100% of revenue goes direct to your bank accounts (less transaction processing fees).
Can existing equipment be used?
Yes, we can usually integrate PARKEZI with existing carpark hardware including ANPR cameras, boom gates and sliding gates. PARKEZI has an open API that allows it to communicate with many other software packages that also support open API communication.
Where can you install?
PARKEZI installs systems in most locations across Australia and New Zealand. Contact the team to discuss your location.
Can you manage our car park?
Yes, we can do this if needed. We can answer queries from the carpark intercom and resolve customer or payment issues 24 hours a day without you needing to be involved.
What can the software do?
The software provides an easy to use interface to configure access control whitelists, time of day pricing structures, multiple zone access control and reporting on revenue, car park activity and other useful data.
What backup options are available?
Backup connectivity and UPS power can be arranged for the site. Discuss your network, power supply and operating requirements with PARKEZI to confirm an appropriate configuration.
How can drivers pay?
Our 'Express Pay' and 'Step & Pay' terminals provide an easy and convenient way for customers to pay for their parking. It is also possible for them to pay you directly and then for their car numberplate to be flagged for instant exit from the carpark. You can also offer customers the ability to pay via a web based browser interface from their mobile or even use a QR code to allow them to exit the carpark.
Can I become a reseller?
We have a reseller program for access and gate automation companies to resell PARKEZI. Contact us for more details.
What is ticketless parking?
Ticketless parking refers to a system where drivers can park their vehicles without the need to obtain a physical ticket or permit. Instead of traditional tickets, the system uses technology like automatic number plate recognition (ANPR) to identify vehicles as they enter and leave parking facilities.
Here's a simplified way it usually works:
Entry: As the vehicle enters the parking lot, its license plate is scanned logging the vehicle's entry time.
Duration: The parking system monitors how long the vehicle remains in the parking facility.
Exit: When the vehicle leaves, its exit time is recorded using the same identification method. The system calculates the parking fee based on the duration of the stay.
Payment: Drivers pay for their parking time via a credit card reader or online payment system.
How can I buy a system?
We offer two options:
Purchase Outright: You can buy the system outright with a one-time payment.
Rent-to-Own: You can rent the system with a plan that allows you to own it after 24 to 36 months.
Where does the revenue go?
Parking revenue is deposited directly into your account. PARKEZI does not take a percentage of parking revenue. Payment processing fees still apply.
Permanent or casual parking?
Permanent Parkers: Individuals who park regularly or long-term, typically holding monthly or annual passes for consistent access. Examples include employees of nearby businesses or residents with reserved parking spaces. This is managed via a cloud-based portal by the parking management company.
Casual Parkers: Individuals who park occasionally, paying for each visit based on time spent parked. PARKEZI software can calculate time spent onsite and then charge using a dynamic pricing structure.

Entry: As the vehicle enters the parking lot, its license plate is scanned logging the vehicle's entry time.
Duration: The parking system monitors how long the vehicle remains in the parking facility.
Exit: When the vehicle leaves, its exit time is recorded using the same identification method. The system calculates the parking fee based on the duration of the stay.
Payment: Drivers pay for their parking time via a credit card reader or online payment system.
How can I buy a system?
We offer two options:
Purchase Outright: You can buy the system outright with a one-time payment.
Rent-to-Own: You can rent the system with a plan that allows you to own it after 24 to 36 months.
Where does the revenue go?
Parking revenue is deposited directly into your account. PARKEZI does not take a percentage of parking revenue. Payment processing fees still apply.
Permanent or casual parking?
Permanent Parkers: Individuals who park regularly or long-term, typically holding monthly or annual passes for consistent access. Examples include employees of nearby businesses or residents with reserved parking spaces. This is managed via a cloud-based portal by the parking management company.
Casual Parkers: Individuals who park occasionally, paying for each visit based on time spent parked. PARKEZI software can calculate time spent onsite and then charge using a dynamic pricing structure.
Show all questions

Product questions
Who is PARKEZI for?
PARKEZI is ideal for parking operators managing parking lots in commercial complexes, hotels, airports, shopping malls, hospitals, and universities. It also serves individuals and corporate clients looking for parking space optimisation.
How is PARKEZI different?
We have designed PARKEZI to be affordable, flexible simple to use and above all, reliable. We can often integrate PARKEZI with existing carpark hardware as well as existing in-house software you may be using. PARKEZI has an easy to use browser based interface that gives you a fast and easy way to configure and update your system and monitor it in real time. 100% of revenue goes direct to your bank accounts (less transaction processing fees).
Can existing equipment be used?
Yes, we can usually integrate PARKEZI with existing carpark hardware including ANPR cameras, boom gates and sliding gates. PARKEZI has an open API that allows it to communicate with many other software packages that also support open API communication.
Where can you install?
PARKEZI installs systems in most locations across Australia and New Zealand. Contact the team to discuss your location.
Can you manage our car park?
Yes, we can do this if needed. We can answer queries from the carpark intercom and resolve customer or payment issues 24 hours a day without you needing to be involved.
What can the software do?
The software provides an easy to use interface to configure access control whitelists, time of day pricing structures, multiple zone access control and reporting on revenue, car park activity and other useful data.
What backup options are available?
Backup connectivity and UPS power can be arranged for the site. Discuss your network, power supply and operating requirements with PARKEZI to confirm an appropriate configuration.
How can drivers pay?
Our 'Express Pay' and 'Step & Pay' terminals provide an easy and convenient way for customers to pay for their parking. It is also possible for them to pay you directly and then for their car numberplate to be flagged for instant exit from the carpark. You can also offer customers the ability to pay via a web based browser interface from their mobile or even use a QR code to allow them to exit the carpark.
Can I become a reseller?
We have a reseller program for access and gate automation companies to resell PARKEZI. Contact us for more details.
What is ticketless parking?
Ticketless parking refers to a system where drivers can park their vehicles without the need to obtain a physical ticket or permit. Instead of traditional tickets, the system uses technology like automatic number plate recognition (ANPR) to identify vehicles as they enter and leave parking facilities.
Here's a simplified way it usually works:
Entry: As the vehicle enters the parking lot, its license plate is scanned logging the vehicle's entry time.
Duration: The parking system monitors how long the vehicle remains in the parking facility.
Exit: When the vehicle leaves, its exit time is recorded using the same identification method. The system calculates the parking fee based on the duration of the stay.
Payment: Drivers pay for their parking time via a credit card reader or online payment system.
How can I buy a system?
We offer two options:
Purchase Outright: You can buy the system outright with a one-time payment.
Rent-to-Own: You can rent the system with a plan that allows you to own it after 24 to 36 months.
Where does the revenue go?
Parking revenue is deposited directly into your account. PARKEZI does not take a percentage of parking revenue. Payment processing fees still apply.
Permanent or casual parking?
Permanent Parkers: Individuals who park regularly or long-term, typically holding monthly or annual passes for consistent access. Examples include employees of nearby businesses or residents with reserved parking spaces. This is managed via a cloud-based portal by the parking management company.
Casual Parkers: Individuals who park occasionally, paying for each visit based on time spent parked. PARKEZI software can calculate time spent onsite and then charge using a dynamic pricing structure.

# SolarGate+ Mobile Boom Gate

Portable, Secure & Smart Access Control for Construction, Events, and Industrial Sites

[1300 309 998](tel:1300309998)
[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

![Solar-powered barrier gate with a stop sign, used for controlling vehicle access.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/SolarGate-HERO-Treated_1.avif)

A Mobile Boom Gate That Works Anywhere 

## A Mobile Boom Gate That Works Anywhere

Not every car park is permanent. Construction sites, events, temporary worksites and overflow areas all need controlled access, but installing fixed infrastructure is not always practical.

SolarGate+ by Parkezi is a mobile boom gate system designed for flexibility. It delivers reliable parking access control without complex electrical installation.

Ideal for locations where:

- Power access is limited

- The site layout changes regularly

- Access control is temporary

- Rapid deployment is required

Because it operates on solar power, it reduces the need for trenching or electrical works — making installation faster and more cost-effective.

![Parkezi ANPR camera for parking access control.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/camera.webp)

Integrated with Parkezi's Smart Parking Systems 

## Integrated with Parkezi’s Smart Parking Systems

SolarGate+ is more than just a physical barrier. It integrates with Parkezi’s ANPR parking system and cloud parking management platform.

That means you can:

- Control access via licence plate recognition

- Keypad access for users

- Built in intercom that can call

- Monitor vehicle movements remotely

- Apply temporary permissions

- Track entry and exit logs

Even in temporary environments, you maintain full visibility and control.

[Learn more about how ANPR integrates with boom gates](https://www.parkezi.com.au/systems/)

![Parkezi ANPR camera installed at a car park entry point.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/ep-why-3.avif)

Why SolarGate+ Makes Sense (black, 4-card) 

## Why SolarGate+ Makes Sense

Fast Setup

Designed for quick deployment. Positioned and configured without major site works.

Solar-Powered Efficiency

Reduces reliance on mains power and lowers energy costs across the site.

Flexible Use Cases

Construction site access, event parking, temporary traffic management, overflow car park control.

Scalable & Relocatable

When the site changes, the gate moves with it. No infrastructure left behind.

Practical for High-Demand Environments 

## Smarter Installation

Construction and event sites often experience unpredictable traffic patterns. SolarGate+ works alongside Parkezi’s parking management software to manage peak flow while maintaining access security.

For operators looking for a portable parking gate system that integrates with modern ticketless technology, SolarGate+ offers a reliable solution.

[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

![Parkezi smart parking ecosystem.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/smarter-parking.webp)

Designed for Australian Conditions 

## Designed for Australian Conditions

Built for durability, SolarGate+ is suited to Australian environments where heat, dust and varying weather conditions are common.

It provides the reliability required for industrial and remote use while remaining simple to operate — so your team can focus on the site, not the gate.

[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

![SolarGate+ mobile boom gate deployed outdoors in an Australian environment.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/SolarGateBG2_1.avif)

Frequently Asked Questions 

## Frequently Asked Questions

Does SolarGate+ require mains power?

No. It operates using solar power, reducing installation complexity.

Can it integrate with ANPR?

Yes. SolarGate+ can be paired with Parkezi's ANPR system for automated access control.

Is it suitable for short-term events?

Yes. It is designed specifically for temporary and flexible deployment.

Can it be relocated easily?

Yes. The system is mobile and can be repositioned as site needs change.

## Need Flexible Access Control?

If your site requires a portable, solar-powered boom gate that works with modern parking automation, SolarGate+ delivers both flexibility and control. Talk to Parkezi about how SolarGate+ can support your project or temporary site.

Can I become a reseller?
We have a reseller program for access and gate automation companies to resell PARKEZI. Contact us for more details.
What is ticketless parking?
Ticketless parking refers to a system where drivers can park their vehicles without the need to obtain a physical ticket or permit. Instead of traditional tickets, the system uses technology like automatic number plate recognition (ANPR) to identify vehicles as they enter and leave parking facilities.
Here's a simplified way it usually works:
Entry: As the vehicle enters the parking lot, its license plate is scanned logging the vehicle's entry time.
Duration: The parking system monitors how long the vehicle remains in the parking facility.
Exit: When the vehicle leaves, its exit time is recorded using the same identification method. The system calculates the parking fee based on the duration of the stay.
Payment: Drivers pay for their parking time via a credit card reader or online payment system.
How can I buy a system?
We offer two options:
Purchase Outright: You can buy the system outright with a one-time payment.
Rent-to-Own: You can rent the system with a plan that allows you to own it after 24 to 36 months.
Where does the revenue go?
Parking revenue is deposited directly into your account. PARKEZI does not take a percentage of parking revenue. Payment processing fees still apply.
Permanent or casual parking?
Permanent Parkers: Individuals who park regularly or long-term, typically holding monthly or annual passes for consistent access. Examples include employees of nearby businesses or residents with reserved parking spaces. This is managed via a cloud-based portal by the parking management company.
Casual Parkers: Individuals who park occasionally, paying for each visit based on time spent parked. PARKEZI software can calculate time spent onsite and then charge using a dynamic pricing structure.

Product questions
Who is PARKEZI for?
PARKEZI is ideal for parking operators managing parking lots in commercial complexes, hotels, airports, shopping malls, hospitals, and universities. It also serves individuals and corporate clients looking for parking space optimisation.
How is PARKEZI different?
We have designed PARKEZI to be affordable, flexible simple to use and above all, reliable. We can often integrate PARKEZI with existing carpark hardware as well as existing in-house software you may be using. PARKEZI has an easy to use browser based interface that gives you a fast and easy way to configure and update your system and monitor it in real time. 100% of revenue goes direct to your bank accounts (less transaction processing fees).
Can existing equipment be used?
Yes, we can usually integrate PARKEZI with existing carpark hardware including ANPR cameras, boom gates and sliding gates. PARKEZI has an open API that allows it to communicate with many other software packages that also support open API communication.
Where can you install?
PARKEZI installs systems in most locations across Australia and New Zealand. Contact the team to discuss your location.
Can you manage our car park?
Yes, we can do this if needed. We can answer queries from the carpark intercom and resolve customer or payment issues 24 hours a day without you needing to be involved.
What can the software do?
The software provides an easy to use interface to configure access control whitelists, time of day pricing structures, multiple zone access control and reporting on revenue, car park activity and other useful data.
What backup options are available?
Backup connectivity and UPS power can be arranged for the site. Discuss your network, power supply and operating requirements with PARKEZI to confirm an appropriate configuration.
How can drivers pay?
Our 'Express Pay' and 'Step & Pay' terminals provide an easy and convenient way for customers to pay for their parking. It is also possible for them to pay you directly and then for their car numberplate to be flagged for instant exit from the carpark. You can also offer customers the ability to pay via a web based browser interface from their mobile or even use a QR code to allow them to exit the carpark.
Can I become a reseller?
We have a reseller program for access and gate automation companies to resell PARKEZI. Contact us for more details.
What is ticketless parking?
Ticketless parking refers to a system where drivers can park their vehicles without the need to obtain a physical ticket or permit. Instead of traditional tickets, the system uses technology like automatic number plate recognition (ANPR) to identify vehicles as they enter and leave parking facilities.
Here's a simplified way it usually works:
Entry: As the vehicle enters the parking lot, its license plate is scanned logging the vehicle's entry time.
Duration: The parking system monitors how long the vehicle remains in the parking facility.
Exit: When the vehicle leaves, its exit time is recorded using the same identification method. The system calculates the parking fee based on the duration of the stay.
Payment: Drivers pay for their parking time via a credit card reader or online payment system.
How can I buy a system?
We offer two options:
Purchase Outright: You can buy the system outright with a one-time payment.
Rent-to-Own: You can rent the system with a plan that allows you to own it after 24 to 36 months.
Where does the revenue go?
Parking revenue is deposited directly into your account. PARKEZI does not take a percentage of parking revenue. Payment processing fees still apply.
Permanent or casual parking?
Permanent Parkers: Individuals who park regularly or long-term, typically holding monthly or annual passes for consistent access. Examples include employees of nearby businesses or residents with reserved parking spaces. This is managed via a cloud-based portal by the parking management company.
Casual Parkers: Individuals who park occasionally, paying for each visit based on time spent parked. PARKEZI software can calculate time spent onsite and then charge using a dynamic pricing structure.

## Why choose PARKEZI ® ?

PARKEZI® streamlines every aspect of your parking operations, from tracking parking usage and revenue, to minimising waiting times and improving traffic flow.

We’ve combined cutting-edge technology with unmatched expertise to deliver custom car park management systems that can be tailored to your unique needs.

## Why choose PARKEZI ® ?

PARKEZI® streamlines every aspect of your parking operations, from tracking parking usage and revenue, to minimising waiting times and improving traffic flow.

We’ve combined cutting-edge technology with unmatched expertise to deliver custom car park management systems that can be tailored to your unique needs.

[![Two overlapping credit cards, one in front of the other, shown as simple white outlines.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/payment-system.svg)

#### Contactless

No ticket? No problem. The ticketless system eliminates delays, providing a fully contactless experience that’s faster, smoother and more convenient for everyone.

Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)
[![](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/Outright.svg)

#### Effortless

Manage your car park from anywhere in the world instantly. Adjust access settings, update your whitelist or monitor real-time data. The cloud-based system puts control at your fingertips.

Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)
[![Parking sign shaped like a surveillance camera with the word "PARKEZI."](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/number-plate-cameras.svg)

#### Reliable

Ensure reliable vehicle recognition with 99.9% camera accuracy. A 4G router guarantees a stable signal between access points, while dual SIM cards offer network reliability.

Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

## A system

## for any scenario

Make car park management one less thing to worry about with a customised system.

[![A parking payment machine with a screen, keypad, card slot, and instructions for use.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/ExpressPay_1.avif)

##### Express Pay

Our hero unit offers drivers easy, convenient access from their vehicle, making it the ideal system for most parking needs.

More Info](https://www.parkezi.com.au/products/express-pay/)
[![Parkezi Step & Pay payment terminal for walk-up parking access.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/StepAndPay_1.avif)

##### Step & Pay

An add-on to Express Pay that can be placed in convenient locations like foyers or lift wells, allowing easy prepayment before entering their vehicle.

More Info](https://www.parkezi.com.au/products/step-and-pay/)
[![Parkezi Scan & Go ANPR camera system for automated parking entry and exit.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/ScanAndGo_1.avif)

##### Scan & Go

A self-contained system for simple entry or exit access control, using an ANPR camera to identify whitelisted vehicles.

More Info](https://www.parkezi.com.au/products/scan-and-go/)
[![Parkezi Scan & Go Mini compact ANPR camera for space-limited parking entrances.](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/scanandgomini.avif)

##### Scan & Go Mini

All the benefits of Scan & Go, but suitable for limited space areas.

More Info](https://www.parkezi.com.au/products/scan-and-go-mini/)

# Knight Frank Chatswood

[1300 309 998](tel:1300309998)
[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

[Copy link](https://www.parkezi.com.au/project/knight-frank-chatswood)

![Knight Frank Chatswood](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688b0ad6ee0f1596f3ce7ffa_672be2129b00a2c562f2779e_2.avif)

![Knight Frank Chatswood](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688b0aa59ae80e4a3bf8cab2_knight-frank-1.avif)

![Knight Frank Chatswood](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688b0aa59807fa2ab291098d_knight-frank-2.avif)

![Knight Frank Chatswood](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688a30331a5888a9d714d498_parklea3.avif)

![Knight Frank Chatswood](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688b0a4b9f4ef91dab91167e_helpst3.avif)

Knight Frank were frustrated by their previous parking system suppliers charging unreasonable fees and delivering a substandard level of service to their clients.  They selected PARKEZI as the replacement system to manage their visitor parking and payments. They particularly liked how all parking revenue goes direct to their accounts, with no commission being charged.

‍

#### Here's how it works:

**Pay on Exit:** Customers are granted access to the parking station for 15mins free parking.  When they are ready to leave, they simply tap their credit card at the Step&Pay pay station or X-press exit pay station to pay their parking fee.

**Instant Digital Receipts:** After payment, users can scan an on-screen QR code for a receipt that can be saved directly to their phone, no paper, no hassle.

**Standalone:**Our system requires only a 240V power connection, no local internet or complex setup. Just our X-press paystation and two reliable [**Benincà Australia**](https://www.linkedin.com/company/beninc%C3%A0-australia/) boom gates.

**Flexible Pass Options:**The Parkly App allows regular customers to buy weekly or monthly passes, generating a daily one-time QR code for entry, easy for users and great for recurring revenue.

**Integration-Friendly:**The system can also be integrated with third-party payment platforms, making it adaptable to a variety of site requirements.

This straightforward setup stops revenue leaks and delivers a frictionless customer experience. Your car park becomes more profitable immediately, without the headaches.

# Parklea Markets

[1300 309 998](tel:1300309998)
[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

[Copy link](https://www.parkezi.com.au/project/parklea-markets)

![Parklea Markets](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688a30336f2d2352d133f5ff_parklea2.avif)

![Parklea Markets](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688a30331a5888a9d714d498_parklea3.avif)

Parklea Markets wanted to raise funds to support their carpark upkeep and maintenance.  They selected PARKEZI as an ideal simple, standalone paid parking system to effortlessly generate revenue, requires no staff, and offers a seamless customer experience.

Here's how it works:

Pay on Exit: Customers can visit the Information desk to pay their fixed parking fee by cash or credit card or simply tap their credit card at the X-press exit pay station.

Instant Digital Receipts: After payment, users can scan an on-screen QR code for a receipt that can be saved directly to their phone, no paper, no hassle.

No Staff Required: Once payment is made, an induction loop automatically lets drivers exit, no staff monitoring necessary.

Standalone: Our system requires only a 240V power connection, no local internet or complex setup. Just our X-press paystation and four reliable [Benincà Australia](https://www.linkedin.com/company/beninc%C3%A0-australia/) boom gates.

This straightforward setup stops revenue leaks and delivers a frictionless customer experience. Your car park becomes profitable immediately, without the headaches.

‍

# Mercure Sydney Macquarie Park

[1300 309 998](tel:1300309998)
[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

[Copy link](https://www.parkezi.com.au/project/mercure-sydney-macquarie-park)

![Mercure Sydney Macquarie Park](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688a3084bf2be8ceb652710f_macpark4.avif)

![Mercure Sydney Macquarie Park](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688a308749da4e8969247cd1_macpark1.avif)

![Mercure Sydney Macquarie Park](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688a308796dc833141cefbb2_macpark2.avif)

![Mercure Sydney Macquarie Park](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688a3087deb474e4036d91f4_macpark3.avif)

Mercure Hotel in Macquarie Park was looking for a robust solution to manage guest parking at their site.

PARKEZI two-way was selected as it offered an easy to use and future proof solution for visitor parking. Mercure uses the PARKEZI software integrated to their reservations system to automatically admit, exit and collect payments for parking from guests staying at the Hotel.

If you’re dealing with complex parking environments and want a seamless experience for every user, let’s chat. Our high‑tech hardware and intelligent software are built to solve the toughest parking challenges.

Knight Frank Chatswood

Knight Frank were frustrated by their previous parking system suppliers charging unreasonable fees and delivering a substandard level of service to their clients.  They selected PARKEZI as the replacement system to manage their visitor parking and payments. They particularly liked how all parking revenue goes direct to their accounts, with no commission being charged.

Here's how it works:

Pay on Exit: Customers are granted access to the parking station for 15mins free parking.  When they are ready to leave, they simply tap their credit card at the Step&Pay pay station or X-press exit pay station to pay their parking fee.

Instant Digital Receipts: After payment, users can scan an on-screen QR code for a receipt that can be saved directly to their phone, no paper, no hassle.

Standalone: Our system requires only a 240V power connection, no local internet or complex setup. Just our X-press paystation and two reliable [Benincà Australia](https://www.linkedin.com/company/beninc%C3%A0-australia/) boom gates.

Flexible Pass Options: The Parkly App allows regular customers to buy weekly or monthly passes, generating a daily one-time QR code for entry, easy for users and great for recurring revenue.

Integration-Friendly: The system can also be integrated with third-party payment platforms, making it adaptable to a variety of site requirements.

This straightforward setup stops revenue leaks and delivers a frictionless customer experience. Your car park becomes more profitable immediately, without the headaches.

# Parkly Penrith

[1300 309 998](tel:1300309998)
[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

[Copy link](https://www.parkezi.com.au/project/parkly-penrith)

![Parkly Penrith](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688a2fb8134c82e9a0472ff5_parkly.avif)

Parkly is an all-in-one managed parking solution to turn a block of land or hardstand into an easy revenue stream. We recently upgraded a run-down car park into a simple, standalone paid parking system that effortlessly generates revenue, requires no staff, and offers a seamless customer experience.

#### Here's how it works:

Pay Before You Enter: Customers simply tap their credit card at our X-press entry pay station to pay a fixed parking fee upfront. This eliminates revenue loss by ensuring every user has paid before entering.

Future-Proofed: No expensive ANPR cameras are required for basic operation, but can be added later for whitelisted users as revenue grows.

Instant Digital Receipts: After payment, users can scan an on-screen QR code for a receipt that can be saved directly to their phone, no paper, no hassle.

No Staff Required: Once payment is made, an induction loop automatically lets drivers exit, no staff monitoring necessary.

Standalone: Our system requires only a 240V power connection, no local internet or complex setup. Just our X-press paystation and two reliable [Benincà Australia](https://www.linkedin.com/company/beninc%C3%A0-australia/) boom gates.

Flexible Pass Options: The Parkly App allows regular customers to buy weekly or monthly passes, generating a daily one-time QR code for entry, easy for users and great for recurring revenue.

Integration-Friendly: The system can also be integrated with third-party payment platforms, making it adaptable to a variety of site requirements.

This straightforward setup stops revenue leaks and delivers a frictionless customer experience. Your car park becomes profitable immediately, without the headaches.

‍

[![Knight Frank Chatswood](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688b0a43737602da8800830b_knight_frank_logo.svg.avif)

Parkly Penrith
Parkly is an all-in-one managed parking solution to turn a block of land or hardstand into an easy revenue stream. We recently upgraded a run-down car park into a simple, standalone paid parking system that effortlessly generates revenue, requires no staff, and offers a seamless customer experience.
Discuss your site
1300 309 998
How it works
Pay Before You Enter: Customers simply tap their credit card at our X-press entry pay station to pay a fixed parking fee upfront. This eliminates revenue loss by ensuring every user has paid before entering.
Future-Proofed: No expensive ANPR cameras are required for basic operation, but can be added later for whitelisted users as revenue grows.
Instant Digital Receipts: After payment, users can scan an on-screen QR code for a receipt that can be saved directly to their phone, no paper, no hassle.
No Staff Required: Once payment is made, an induction loop automatically lets drivers exit, no staff monitoring necessary.
Standalone: Our system requires only a 240V power connection, no local internet or complex setup. Just our X-press paystation and two reliable
Benincà Australia
boom gates.
Flexible Pass Options: The Parkly App allows regular customers to buy weekly or monthly passes, generating a daily one-time QR code for entry, easy for users and great for recurring revenue.
Integration-Friendly: The system can also be integrated with third-party payment platforms, making it adaptable to a variety of site requirements.
This straightforward setup stops revenue leaks and delivers a frictionless customer experience.

# Quest Apartments

[1300 309 998](tel:1300309998)
[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

[Copy link](https://www.parkezi.com.au/project/quest-apartments)

![Quest Apartments](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688a2f414154271c64b038dd_quest3.avif)

![Quest Apartments](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688a2f44f44882e875664d30_quest1.avif)

![Quest Apartments](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/688a2f44562328b95eeb5386_quest2.avif)

At Quest Apartments & Northies Hotel, one car park caters to long‑term residents, short‑term serviced apartments, four different businesses, staff, and hotel patrons.

#### The challenge?

Each group has unique needs, and access must be seamless.

Residents need guaranteed spaces and restricted vehicle counts.

Businesses and staff have allocated spaces.

Hotel patrons must be able to come and go easily, with a quick way to validate parking.

#### Here’s how we solved it.

We implemented a PARKEZI Paid system that gives the site owner complete control. Multiple user levels and access types were configured:

Residents: Whitelisted with a set number of vehicles, so no overflow.

Businesses & Staff: Whitelisted with allocated spaces.

Hotel Patrons: Parking is validated quickly via an iPad on the reception desk, allowing staff to search a plate number and validate it for free exit.

For patrons who forget their plate? They can be sent a one‑time QR code via SMS from the iPad or handed a printed coupon from a handheld printer. This can be scanned at the X‑press Exit Station, matching the plate to the parking session and marking it as validated.

For the public: The car park can also be a paid facility, with a credit card option at the exit for those enjoying a day at nearby Cronulla Beach.

#### Why this matters:

The car park is the first and last experience for visitors. It should be seamless, stress‑free, and practically invisible and that’s exactly what we’ve delivered for Quest Apartments & Northies Hotel.

If you’re dealing with complex parking environments and want a seamless experience for every user, let’s chat. Our high‑tech hardware and intelligent software are built to solve the toughest parking challenges.

# Tasman Holiday Parks

[1300 309 998](tel:1300309998)
[Enquire](https://www.parkezi.com.au/contact/)

[Copy link](https://www.parkezi.com.au/project/tasman-holiday-parks)

![Tasman Holiday Parks](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/6889e9e862f84d507b9fefc6_tasman1.avif)

![Tasman Holiday Parks](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/6889e9ec1c2eb4a2fc4de172_tasman2.avif)

![Tasman Holiday Parks](https://www.parkezi.com.au/images/6889e9eb1443e0fe12d74394_tasman3.avif)

Tasman Holiday Parks selected PARKEZI for their Wiseman's Ferry site which has over 100 cabins. Managing guests (and their friends) coming and going could have been a nightmare. But now?

- Staff create a ParkEzi user account for each cabin

- Cabin owners or tenants receive a login link via email so they can register up to 4 number plates for their family and friends.

Because park residents manage their own bookings, there is no admin follow-up needed. No spreadsheets. No reception calls. No stress. It just works.

**How the one-way system works:**

- A camera scans licence plates at the entry and checks them against the cabin’s whitelist

- An in-ground loop at the exit automatically opens the gate

- Every vehicle entry is logged in the PARKEZI dashboard

Optional add-ons for PARKEZI one-way include intercoms, keypads, swipe cards, and QR code scanners for one-time contractor or visitor access

This setup is completely standalone. Just install the Scan & Go Mini, connect 240V power, aim the camera, and you're ready. The camera talks to the PARKEZI software using local ethernet with a 4G data link installed as backup.

PARKEZI - Parking made simple, easy and effective.
